381: async function load() {
382:   // 영속 상태를 읽기 전에는 화면 렌더/이벤트가 기본값을 저장하지 못하도록 부팅 장벽을 둡니다.
383:   // 로컬 IPC 파일 읽기는 외부 네트워크와 무관하므로 반드시 먼저 완료시킵니다.
384:   try {
385:     const saved = window.schoolBoard?.loadState
386:       ? await withStartupTimeout(window.schoolBoard.loadState(), 1800, null)
387:       : JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("gyomuon-state") || "null");

393:

395:     normalizeSavedState();

399:     const settingsSnapshot=state?.settings ? structuredClone(state.settings) : structuredClone(baseState.settings);

1765:     const no=String(row.studentNo||'').replace(/\D/g,'');
1766:     const inferredGrade=no?Number(no[0]):Number(String(row.className||'').match(/^([123])[- ]/)?.[1]||1);
1767:     const grade=Number(row.grade||inferredGrade||1);
1768:     if(year&&term.year&&year!==term.year)return false;
1769:     if(grade!==term.grade)return false;
1770:     if(term.semester&&sem&&sem!==term.semester)return false;
1771:     if(term.semester&&!sem){

2918: function inputCenterHumanReportText(value=''){
2919:   const source=String(value||'').trim();if(!source)return '';
2920:   try{
2921:     const meta=JSON.parse(source);
2922:     const fields=Array.isArray(meta?.fields)?meta.fields:Array.isArray(meta)?meta:[];
2923:     const skip=/^(연락처|전화번호|휴대폰|서명|이메일|학번|성명|이름|학년|반|수정시간|제출시간|제출일시)$/;
2924:     const pairs=fields.map((f,i)=>({label:String(f?.label||`문항 ${i+1}`).trim(),value:String(f?.value||'').trim()})).filter(x=>x.value&&!skip.test(x.label));
2925:     if(pairs.length)return pairs.map(x=>`[${x.label||'문항'}]\n${x.value}`).join('\n\n');
2926:   }catch{}
2927:   return source;
2928: }

3016: function submissionDateText(value){
3017:   const raw=String(value??"").trim();
3018:   if(!raw)return "-";
3019:   const serial=Number(raw);
3020:   let date=null;
3021:   if(Number.isFinite(serial)&&serial>20000&&serial<100000)date=new Date(Date.UTC(1899,11,30)+serial*86400000);
3022:   else{
3023:     const parsed=Date.parse(raw.replace(/오전/g,"AM").replace(/오후/g,"PM"));
3024:     if(Number.isFinite(parsed))date=new Date(parsed);
3025:   }
3026:   if(!date||Number.isNaN(date.getTime()))return raw;
3027:   return `${date.getFullYear()}.${String(date.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0")}.${String(date.getDate()).padStart(2,"0")}`;
3028: }

3932: function accessAccountRole(account){

4329: function internalNineGradeCut(percentile){
4330:   if(percentile<=4)return 1;
4331:   if(percentile<=11)return 2;
4332:   if(percentile<=23)return 3;
4333:   if(percentile<=40)return 4;
4334:   if(percentile<=60)return 5;
4335:   if(percentile<=77)return 6;
4336:   if(percentile<=89)return 7;
4337:   if(percentile<=96)return 8;
4338:   return 9;
4339: }

7941: function noticeCollectionState(item){

7943:   const pending=[];

8712: function sourceReferenceRowsMarkup(rows, type) {
8713:   if (!rows.length) return '<div class="outing-empty">검색 결과가 없습니다.</div>';
8714:   if (type === "homeroom") {
8715:     const grades = [...new Set(rows.map((row) => String(row[0])))].sort();
8716:     const gradeHeads = { "1": "홍석민", "2": "윤용철", "3": "정조일" };

8779: function parseReferencePaste(text, type) {
8780:   const headers = {
8781:     approval: ["업무분류", "업무 종류", "결재라인", "비고"],
8782:     homeroom: ["학년", "반", "담임", "부담임"],
8783:     extension: ["구역", "이름", "시설", "자리", "대표번호", "내선"],
8784:   }[type] || [];
8785:   const widths = { approval: 3, homeroom: 4, extension: 4 };
8786:   return String(text || "").split(/\r?\n/).map((line) => {
8787:     const cells = (line.includes("\t") ? line.split("\t") : line.split(/\s{2,}|,/))
8788:       .map((cell) => String(cell || "").trim());
8789:     while (cells.length && !cells[cells.length - 1]) cells.pop();
8790:     return cells.slice(0, widths[type] || cells.length);
8791:   }).filter((row) => {
8792:     const joined = row.join(" ");
8793:     return row.filter(Boolean).length >= 2 && !headers.some((header) => joined === header || joined.startsWith(`${header} `));
8794:   });
8795: }

8808: }

8902: function printTimetable() {
8903:   const popup = window.open("", "_blank", "width=1050,height=820");
8904:   if (!popup) return toast("인쇄 창을 열 수 없습니다.");

10100:     const run = () => {

10106:       });
10107:     };

10110:       if (input) input.value = "";

10112:     };
10113:     return run;

11681: async function loadWeather() {
11682:   try {
11683:     const response = await fetch(
11684:       "https://api.open-meteo.com/v1/forecast?latitude=36.6424&longitude=127.4890&current=temperature_2m,apparent_temperature,relative_humidity_2m,weather_code,precipitation,wind_speed_10m&hourly=temperature_2m,relative_humidity_2m,precipitation_probability,weather_code,wind_speed_10m&daily=weather_code,temperature_2m_max,temperature_2m_min,precipitation_probability_max&timezone=Asia%2FSeoul&forecast_days=8",
11685:     );
11686:     if (!response.ok) throw new Error("weather unavailable");
11687:     const data = await response.json();
11688:     const now = new Date();
11689:     const currentHour = now.getHours();
11690:     const hourKey = `${now.getFullYear()}-${String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}T${String(currentHour).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
11691:     const currentIndex = Math.max(0, data.hourly.time.indexOf(hourKey));
11692:     weatherState = {
11693:       temp: Math.round(data.current.temperature_2m),
11694:       feels: Math.round(data.current.apparent_temperature),
11695:       code: data.current.weather_code,
11696:       humidity: data.current.relative_humidity_2m ?? 0,
11697:       wind: Math.round((data.current.wind_speed_10m ?? 0) * 10) / 10,
11698:       rain: data.hourly.precipitation_probability[currentIndex] ?? 0,
11699:       precipitation: data.current.precipitation ?? 0,
11700:       daily: data.daily.time.map((time, index) => ({
11701:         time,
11702:         code: data.daily.weather_code[index],
11703:         max: Math.round(data.daily.temperature_2m_max[index]),
11704:         min: Math.round(data.daily.temperature_2m_min[index]),
11705:         rain: data.daily.precipitation_probability_max[index] ?? 0,
11706:       })),
11707:       hourly: data.hourly.time.slice(currentIndex, currentIndex + 8).map((time, offset) => {
11708:         const index = currentIndex + offset;

2331:   ipcMain.handle("app:applyUpdate", async (_event, payload = {}) => {
2332:     try {
2333:       if (process.platform !== "win32") return {ok:false,reason:"현재 자동 업데이트는 Windows 배포판에서만 지원합니다."};
2334:       const downloadUrl=String(payload.downloadUrl||"").trim();
2335:       const expectedSha256=String(payload.sha256||"").trim().toLowerCase();
2336:       const version=String(payload.version||"").trim();
2337:       if(!/^https:\/\//i.test(downloadUrl)) return {ok:false,reason:"HTTPS 업데이트 파일 주소가 필요합니다."};
2338:       if(!/^\d+\.\d+\.\d+/.test(version)) return {ok:false,reason:"업데이트 버전 정보가 올바르지 않습니다."};
2339:       if(!/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(expectedSha256)) return {ok:false,reason:"안전한 자동 업데이트를 위해 SHA-256 값이 필요합니다."};
2340:       const updateRoot=path.join(stableUserDataRoot(),"updates",version);
2341:       const zipPath=path.join(updateRoot,`UEP-${version}.zip`);
2342:       const stagePath=path.join(updateRoot,"stage");
2343:       await fs.rm(updateRoot,{recursive:true,force:true}); await fs.mkdir(updateRoot,{recursive:true});
2344:       // Download the release asset from Electron's main process.
2345:       // Node global fetch was unreliable on some school Windows networks ("fetch failed"),
2346:       // while Electron net.fetch uses Chromium's network stack and follows GitHub redirects.
2347:       let response;
2348:       try {
2349:         response=await net.fetch(downloadUrl,{
2350:           method:"GET",
2351:           redirect:"follow",
2352:           headers:{
2353:             "user-agent":`UEP/${app.getVersion()} AutoUpdate`,
2354:             "accept":"application/octet-stream",
2355:             "cache-control":"no-cache, no-store, max-age=0",
2356:             "pragma":"no-cache"
2357:           }
2358:         });
2359:       } catch(downloadError) {
2360:         return {ok:false,reason:`업데이트 파일 다운로드 연결 실패 · ${String(downloadError?.message||downloadError)}`};
2361:       }
2362:       if(!response.ok) return {ok:false,reason:`업데이트 파일 다운로드 실패 (${response.status})`};
2363:       let bytes;

2412: 
2413:       // Always resolve the freshest manifest we can find. During school-wide use,
2414:       // raw.githubusercontent.com / CDN caches can be stale or rate-limited, so:
2415:       //   1) derive GitHub Contents API + raw + jsDelivr candidates,
2416:       //   2) cache-bust non-API candidates,
2417:       //   3) read every reachable candidate and choose the HIGHEST semantic version.
2418:       const stamp=Date.now();
2419:       const candidates=[];
2420:       const push=(url,kind)=>{if(url && !candidates.some(x=>x.url===url))candidates.push({url,kind});};
2421:       let canonical=rawUrl;
2422:       try {
2423:         const u=new URL(rawUrl);
2424:         u.search=""; u.hash="";
2425:         canonical=u.toString();
2426:         if(u.hostname.toLowerCase()==="raw.githubusercontent.com"){
2427:           const parts=u.pathname.split('/').filter(Boolean);
2428:           if(parts.length>=4){
2429:             const [owner,repo,ref,...fileParts]=parts;
2430:             const filePath=fileParts.join('/');
2431:             push(`https://api.github.com/repos/${owner}/${repo}/contents/${filePath}?ref=${encodeURIComponent(ref)}`,'github-api');
2432:             push(`${u.toString()}?uep=${stamp}`,'github-raw');
2433:             push(`https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/${owner}/${repo}@${ref}/${filePath}?uep=${stamp}`,'jsdelivr');
2434:           }
2435:         } else {
2436:           push(`${u.toString()}${u.search?'&':'?'}uep=${stamp}`,'direct');
2437:         }
2438:       } catch {
2439:         push(`${rawUrl}${rawUrl.includes('?')?'&':'?'}uep=${stamp}`,'direct');
2440:       }
2441:       if(!candidates.length) push(canonical,'direct');

265:   const masterSpreadsheetId =
266:     text_(config.MASTER_SPREADSHEET_ID) ||
267:     text_(config.STUDENT_MASTER_SPREADSHEET_ID) ||
268:     text_(config.TARGET_DB_SPREADSHEET_ID) ||
269:     UEP_DORM_PROCESSOR.MASTER_SPREADSHEET_ID_DEFAULT;
270: 
271:   const masterSs = SpreadsheetApp.openById(masterSpreadsheetId);
272:   const studentSheetName = text_(config.STUDENT_MASTER_SHEET) || '02_학생마스터';
273:   const dormSheetName = text_(config.DORM_MASTER_SHEET) || '20_학사생마스터';
274:   const studentSheet = masterSs.getSheetByName(studentSheetName);
275:   const dormSheet = masterSs.getSheetByName(dormSheetName);
276: 
277:   if (!studentSheet) throw new Error(`학생마스터를 찾을 수 없습니다: ${studentSheetName}`);
278:   if (!dormSheet) throw new Error(`학사생마스터를 찾을 수 없습니다: ${dormSheetName}`);
279: 
280:   return {
281:     config,
282:     sourceRows: sourceBundle.table,
283:     sourceMode: sourceBundle.mode,
284:     students: readStudentMaster_(studentSheet),
285:     dormStudents: readDormMaster_(dormSheet),
286:     masterSpreadsheetId,
287:   };
288: }
289: function configureSourceFormSafety_() {
290:   const config = getConfig_();
291:   const sourceSs = SpreadsheetApp.openById(requiredConfig_(config, 'SOURCE_FORM_SPREADSHEET_ID'));
292:   const sourceSheet = sourceSs.getSheetByName(requiredConfig_(config, 'SOURCE_FORM_SHEET'));
293:   if (!sourceSheet) throw new Error('원본 학사외출 탭을 찾을 수 없습니다.');
294: 
295:   const formUrl = sourceSheet.getFormUrl();
296:   if (!formUrl) {
297:     throw new Error('학사외출 응답 탭에 연결된 구글폼 URL을 찾지 못했습니다.');
298:   }
299: 
300:   const form = FormApp.openByUrl(formUrl);
301:   form.setAllowResponseEdits(false);
302:   setConfigValue_('SOURCE_FORM_ID', form.getId());
303:   setConfigValue_('SOURCE_READ_MODE', 'FORM_DIRECT');
304:   setConfigValue_('ALLOW_SHEET_FALLBACK', 'FALSE');
305:   setConfigValue_('FORM_RESPONSE_EDITING', 'DISABLED');
306: }
307: 
308: function readDormOutingSource_(sourceSheet, config) {
309:   const mode = text_(config.SOURCE_READ_MODE || 'FORM_DIRECT').toUpperCase();
310:   if (mode === 'FORM_DIRECT') {
311:     const formUrl = sourceSheet.getFormUrl();
312:     if (formUrl) {
313:       const form = FormApp.openByUrl(formUrl);
314:       const table = readFormResponsesAsTable_(form);
315:       syncDormOutingCollectionSheet_(table);
316:       return {
317:         mode: 'FORM_DIRECT',
318:         table,
319:       };
320:     }
321: 
322:     const allowFallback = text_(config.ALLOW_SHEET_FALLBACK).toUpperCase() === 'TRUE';
323:     if (!allowFallback) {
324:       throw new Error('연결된 구글폼을 찾지 못해 원본 응답을 직접 읽을 수 없습니다.');
325:     }
326:   }
327: 
328:   return {
329:     mode: 'SHEET_FALLBACK',
330:     table: readTableFromRow1_(sourceSheet),
331:   };
332: }
333: 
334: function readFormResponsesAsTable_(form) {
335:   const responses = form.getResponses();
336:   const titles = [];
337: 
338:   responses.forEach((response) => {
339:     response.getItemResponses().forEach((itemResponse) => {
340:       const title = text_(itemResponse.getItem().getTitle());
341:       if (title && !titles.includes(title)) titles.push(title);
342:     });
343:   });
